Gradient:
Given a=softmax θ, b, w=exp(λ b - max)/sum (weights). grad_a = 2 corr(a, w) (as derived), where corr[i]=∑ a_m w_{m+i}. Then grad_θ = a ∘ grad_a - (∑ a_i grad_a_i) a. Because derivative of softmax: δa_j/δθ_i = a_i(δ_{ji}-a_j). Then ∑ grad_a_j δa_j = a_i(grad_a_i - ∑a grad_a). Yes.
Need compute corr length n using FFT size L >= n + len(w) -1 = n + (2n-1) -1 =3n-1? Wait corr[i]=∑_{m=0}^{n-1} a_m w_{m+i}. w index from 0 to 2n-2. For i up to n-1, m+i up to 3n-3? For linear correlation where w longer than a, the circular FFT length L must be at least len(a)+len(w)-1 = n + (2n-1) -1 =3n-2 to avoid wrap. For n=3809, L=11426, next power 16384. Good.
To compute corr: use FFT of a zero padded L and w zero padded L. corr[i]=? If A=FFT(a), W=FFT(w). `np.ifft(A * np.conjugate(W))` yields circular correlation r[k]=∑_{m} a_m w_{(m+k) mod L}. For k=0..n-1, since L large enough, m+k< L, no wrap, so r[k]=corr. Great. Then grad_a=2*r.

Let's design a pipeline:

A. Fast evaluation:
```
def convolve_fft(a):
    n=a.size
    L=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:2*n-1]
    # due roundoff small negatives clamp to 0? For objective max, clip small negative
    if b.max()<0? b=np.clip(b,0,None)
    return b
```
For n up to maybe 10000, L power <=32768.

B. Soft objective and gradient:
```
def softmax_obj_grad(theta, lam):
    a=np.exp(theta - theta.max()); a/=a.sum()
    b=convolve_fft(a)
    if b.size==0...
    lb=lam*b
    m=lb.max()
    w=np.exp(lb-m); s=w.sum()
    val=(m + np.log(s))/lam
    # gradient wrt a
    # corr via fft size Lcorr maybe next power n+2n-2?
    Lc=1 << (3*n-2).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft? np.fft.fft for complex maybe; use rfft? w real, a real.
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, Lc); W=np.fft.rfft(w, Lc)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(A * np.conj(W), Lc)[:n]
    grad_a=2*corr
    s_a=dot(a, grad_a)
    grad=(a*(grad_a - s_a)) # because a_i grad_a_i - sum*a_i? Actually a*(grad_a - sum_a*1)
    # L-BFGS expects grad wrt theta. It will use. Ensure finite.
    return val, grad
```

def _convolve_fft(a):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    L=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    # clean small negatives
    c[c<0] = 0? But for gradient weights b can be slightly negative. Use clip min 0.
    return c
```

Gradient for restricted softmax:
```
def _softmax_from_restricted(phi, n):
    if n==1: return np.ones(1)
    x=np.empty(n)
    x[:-1]=phi
    x[-1]=0.0
    # stable softmax
    x -= np.max(x)
    ex=np.exp(x)
    s=ex.sum()
    if s<=0: ex=np.exp(np.linspace?); s=...
    return ex/s

def _lbfgs_fun_grad(phi, n, lam):
    a=_softmax_from_restricted(phi,n)
    c=_convolve_fft(a)
    x=lam*c
    m=x.max(); if not finite: return 1e300, zeros
    w=np.exp(x-m); s=w.sum(); w/=s
    val=(m+math.log(s))/lam
    Lc=1 << (3*n-2).bit_length() # maybe too large for small; if n=1 special
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, Lc)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w, Lc)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conjugate(fw), Lc)[:n]
    grad_a=2.0*corr
    alpha=float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_full=a*(grad_a-alpha)
    grad=grad_full[:-1]
    if not all finite: val+=1e6, grad=0
    return val, grad
```
Is Lc formula enough? corr[i]=∑_{m=0}^{n-1}a_m w_{m+i}; max index m+i =2n-2. The circular correlation `ifft(A * conj(W))` with zero padding length L returns r[k] = ∑_{m=0}^{L-1} a_m W_{(m+k) mod L}. For k=0..n-1, m up to n-1, m+k up to 2n-2. If L > 2n-2, no wrap. Need L >= (len(a)-1)+(len(w)-1)+1 = n-1+2n-2+1=3n-2. Yes. For n=3809 Lc=11426 ->16384. For n=256 Lc=766 ->1024.

Let's define `_lbfgs_fgv(phi)`:
```
def _lbfgs_fgv(phi):
    n=_LBFGS_N
    lam=_LBFGS_LAM
    phi=np.asarray(phi,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if phi.size != n-1: phi=phi[:n-1] or pad?
    a=_softmax_restricted(phi,n)
    c=_convolve_fft(a)
    x=lam*c; m=x.max()
    if not np.isfinite(m): return 1e300, np.zeros(n-1)
    exp=np.exp(x-m); s=exp.sum()
    if s<=0: ...
    w=exp/s
    val=(m + math.log(s))/lam
    # exact score
    mx=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
    score=2*n*mx
    if np.isfinite(score) and score < _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE:
       _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE = score
       _LBFGS_BEST_SEQ = a.copy()
    # gradient
    Lc=...
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,Lc); fw=np.fft.rfft(w,Lc)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fw),Lc)[:n]
    ga=2*corr
    alpha=np.dot(a,ga)
    gf=a*(ga-alpha)
    grad=gf[:-1]
    if not finite: grad=zeros; val=1e300
    return val, grad
```

    sort, return top k sequences

Let's write `_coord_update` carefully:
```
def _coordinate_update(p, c, score, i, grid_count, refine_count):
    n=p.size
    old=float(p[i])
    if not np.isfinite(old) or old<0: old=0
    D=1.0-old
    if D<0: D=0
    # affected slice
    k0=int(i); k1=k0+n
    ck=c[k0:k1]
    coeff=2.0*p.copy()
    const=-2.0*old*p
    mid=k0
    coeff[mid]=0.0
    const[mid]=-old*old
    max_unaff=0.0
    if k0>0:
       m=float(np.max(c[:k0])); if finite max
    if k1 < c.size:
       m=float(np.max(c[k1:])); ...
    # samples
    gc=max(5,int(grid_count))
    if old > 0:
       ratios=np.logspace(-10.0, 10.0, gc)
       xs=old*ratios
    else:
       xs=np.logspace(-10.0, 3.0, gc)
    xs=np.concatenate((xs, np.array([0.0, old], dtype=np.float64)))
    xs=np.asarray(xs, dtype=np.float64)
    xs=xs[np.isfinite(xs) & (xs>=0.0) & (xs<=1.0e6)]
    if xs.size==0: return ...
    # evaluate
    aff = ck[None,:] + coeff[None,:]*xs[:,None] + const[None,:]
    aff[:, mid] += xs*xs
    max_aff=np.max(aff, axis=1)
    maxc=np.maximum(max_unaff, max_aff)
    S=D+xs
    vals=np.full(xs.size, inf)
    valid=S>1e-15
    vals[valid]=(2.0*n)*maxc[valid]/(S[valid]*S[valid])
    idx=int(np.argmin(vals))
    best_x=float(xs[idx]); best_val=float(vals[idx])
    tol=1e-12*max(1.0, float(score))
    if not finite best_val or best_val >= score - tol: return p,c,score,False
    # refine
    if refine_count>0:
       if idx>0: left=float(xs[idx-1])
       else: left=0.0
       if idx<xs.size-1: right=float(xs[idx+1])
       else: right=best_x*10.0
       if best_x > 0:
          local=np.logspace(math.log10(max(best_x,1e-300))-1.0, math.log10(max(best_x,1e-300))+1.0, refine_count)
       else:
          lo=max(left,0.0); hi=right if right>0 else 1e-6
          if hi<=lo: hi=lo+1e-12
          local=np.logspace(max(-12, math.log10(max(hi,1e-300)))-1, 0, refine_count) maybe
       local=np.concatenate((local, [left,best_x,right]))
       local=np.unique(...)
       evaluate local...
```
Need avoid math.log10(0), use if.
For refine around best_x, it may find better. If best_x=0, maybe optimum zero; local linear from 0 to right: `np.linspace(0, right, refine_count)` maybe better than log. Use both:
```
if best_x==0:
    local=np.linspace(0.0, max(right,1e-12), refine_count)
else:
    local=best_x*np.logspace(-1,1,refine_count)
```
Add left/right.
After refine evaluate similarly. We can factor evaluation into inline to avoid nested function. Write a small local block duplicated.

After selecting best, update:
```
S=D+best_x; S2=S*S
c_new=c/S2
aff_best=ck + coeff*best_x + const
aff_best[mid]+=best_x*best_x
c_new[k0:k1]=aff_best/S2
p_new=p/S
p_new[i]=best_x/S
# clean and renormalize
p_new=np.clip(p_new,0,None); c_new=np.clip(c_new,0,None)
s=float(np.sum(p_new)); if s<=0: return old
p_new/=s; c_new/=s*s
score_new=2*n*max(c_new)
if score_new < score - tol: return p_new,c_new,score_new,True
```
But note `c_new=c/S2` scales all coefficients, including unaffected; good. For affected, computed unscaled with original p and x; yes.

Potential overflow if x up to 1e6 and p ~1, x^2 1e12, okay. c_new maybe finite.

Now, `score` in CD is exact full score. `_coord_update` value `(2n)*maxc/S^2` exact new score if p sum after scaling? Since p_new normalized to sum1, yes. It uses maxc including max_unaff unaffected; but c_new for unaffected scaled. Good.

def _poly_start(n, rng):
    p=np.ones(n)

      x=np.linspace(-1,1,n)
      p = np.ones(n)

Now, `_score_direct` function:
```
def _score_direct(a):
    a=_normalize_array(a)
    if a.size==0 return inf
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    if c.size==0 return inf
    mx=np.max(c)
    if not finite return inf
    return 2*a.size*max(0,mx)
```
For external, we use list so clamping etc. But our score direct no clamp max1000; values <=1.

Memory: `_lbfgs_fgv` calls `_convolve_fft` and then rfft of a/w again. It has a already; we can avoid second rfft by using a's FFT? For conv, L_conv=nextpow(2n-1) (8192), for corr Lc=nextpow(3n-2) (16384), different lengths; cannot reuse. Could use same Lc for conv to reuse? If we compute fa_Lc and fw_Lc, Lc >=2n-1, so can compute circular conv from fa_Lc* fa_Lc and take first 2n-1. This saves one FFT of a (different length). But Lc larger; one a FFT size16384 plus conv from it, and w FFT size16384, corr product. We need fa for conv and corr; same type. Let's do:
```
Lc=nextpow(3n-2)
fa=np.fft.rfft(a,Lc)
conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,Lc)[:2n-1]
# but Lc may be larger than 2n-1; circular convolution length Lc, since Lc >= 2n-1, first 2n-1 correct.
fw=np.fft.rfft(w,Lc)
corr=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fw),Lc)[:n]
```
This uses 2 FFTs (a,w) instead of 3. However conv via Lc (16384) larger than 8192, a bit slower but okay. Simpler to keep separate? For performance, reuse. For gradient, we need conv and corr. Let's create a helper `_conv_corr_weights(a,w,Lc=None)`:
```
def _fft_conv_and_corr(a, w):
    n=a.size
    Lrc=1 << (max(2*n-1, 3*n-2)-1).bit_length() if n>1 else 1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, Lrc)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, Lrc)[:2*n-1]
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w, Lrc)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conjugate(fw), Lrc)[:n]
    return conv, corr
```
But for score starts without w, just conv. Use `_convolve_fft` with smaller L. In LBFGS, we can inline compute. However `_convolve_fft` called inside `_lbfgs_fgv` then another FFT for corr. Reusing is better. Let's implement `_fft_conv_corr(a, w)`.
Need if n=1: conv=[a0*a0], corr=[a0*w0]? For gradient not used but handle.
For n>1, Lp=`1 << (3*n-2 -1).bit_length()`? The required length for corr is 3n-2; for conv 2n-1. If `size=3*n-2`, next pow. But for n=2, size=4, L=4. corr max index 2? enough. Use `required = max(2*n - 1, 3*n - 2)`; `L = 1 << (required - 1).bit_length()`.
Then `fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L); conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:2*n-1]`; `fw=np.fft.rfft(w, L); corr=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conjugate(fw), L)[:n]`.
The corr formula with `fa` and `fw`: If using rfft, product `fa * fw.conj()` and irfft gives aut? Need confirm: rfft real. For cross-correlation r[k]=∑ a_m w_{m+k}. Circular correlation = ifft( A * conj(W) ). In numpy rfft: `irfft(A * W.conj())` should work. Test with a=[1,2], w=[3,4,5] L? r[0]=1*3+2*4=11; r[1]=1*4+2*5=14. A=fft(a), W=fft(w). ifft(A*conj(W))[0]=∑ a_m w_m? 1*3+2*4=11. yes. Good.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    global _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE, _LBFGS_BEST_SEQ
    start=time.monotonic()
    budget=max(1.0,float(budget_s))
    deadline=start+max(10.0,budget-10.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    final_len=3809
    candidates=[]
    # helpers add with score fast
    def no closures... use top-level _add_candidate(candidates, seq, score)
    # add height
    try:
        hs=_clean_array(height_sequence_1)
        if hs.size>0:
            sc=_score_fast(hs)
            _add_candidate(candidates, hs, sc)
    except: pass
    # fallback
    fallback=_two_level(final_len) # maybe better baseline?
    _add_candidate(candidates, fallback, _score_fast(fallback))
    # If little time left return best
    if time.monotonic()>deadline-20: return list(best)
    # Fourier scan full
    fourier_deadline = min(deadline, start+120)
    for seq in _scan_fourier_starts(final_len, rng, k=20, deadline=fourier_deadline): add
    # initial pool full
    pool_deadline = min(deadline, start+180)
    for seq in _make_initial_pool(final_len, rng, limit=30, deadline=pool_deadline): add
    # prototype multi-res
    proto_deadline = min(deadline, start+700) # reserve full
    if time < proto_deadline:
       m_list=[16,32,64,128,256]
       for m in m_list:
          if time>=proto_deadline: break
          # fourier prototype scan
          for seq in _scan_fourier_starts(m, rng, k=8, deadline=...):
             # quick LBFGS if time
             p=_lbfgs_quick(seq, ...)
             # expand to final and add; also add prototype if maybe short
             add p; expand and add
          # random pool
          ...
```

Now, candidate expansion: For prototype sequences not full, we can add both short and expanded. For short, final may choose if better. For expansion, use `_expand_to_length_smooth`.
```
def _expand_to_length_smooth(proto, target_len):
    m=proto.size
    if m==target_len: return proto
    if m==1: return np.full(target_len, proto[0]/target_len)
    density=proto*m
    x_new=(np.arange(target_len)+0.5)/target_len
    x_old=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
    density_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, density)
    a=density_new
    a=np.clip(a,0,None); s=a.sum(); if s<=0: uniform; return a/s
```
This preserves sum approximate. Good.

Coordinate polish for short prototypes cheap; can run more cycles.

Detailed functions:

```
def _clean_array(arr):
    try:
        a=np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return np.ones(1)
    if a.ndim !=1: a=a.ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    # if all zero -> ones? For clean height maybe if sum0.
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0:
        # keep zeros? For starts we usually normalize to uniform if zero.
        # For height_sequence, if all zero invalid; return uniform length? maybe original length.
        return np.ones(a.size)
    return a
```
For cleaning, if input all zero, maybe return zeros? But normalize later. For height, if all zero score inf. We can if s<=0 return np.ones(a.size). Good.

`_convolve_fft(a)` for small n maybe use direct to avoid FFT overhead and negative:
```
def _convolve_fft(a):
    n=a.size
    if n<=64:
        c=np.convolve(a,a)
        return np.clip(c,0,None)
    L=1 << (2*n - 2).bit_length()? required=2n-1, next pow of required: 1 << (required-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.clip(c,0,None)
    return c
```
Need if required=1 bit_length? use function.

`_fft_conv_corr(a,w)` maybe not separate.

`_lbfgs_fgv`:
```
def _lbfgs_fgv(phi):
    global _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE, _LBFGS_BEST_SEQ
    n=_LBFGS_N
    lam=_LBFGS_LAM
    if n<=1:
       return 2.0, np.zeros(0)
    phi=np.asarray(phi,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if phi.size != n-1:
       tmp=np.zeros(n-1)
       m=min(phi.size,n-1); tmp[:m]=phi[:m]; phi=tmp
    a=_softmax_restricted(phi,n)
    required=3*n-2
    L=1 << (required-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    c=np.clip(c,0,None)
    mx=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    nx=n
    ex=lambda? no
    x=lam*c
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return 1e300, zeros
    m=np.max(x)
    exp=np.exp(x-m)
    s=exp.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0: exp=np.ones_like(c)/c.size; s=c.size
    w=exp/s
    val=(m + math.log(s))/lam
    # update best
    if np.isfinite(mx):
      score=2.0*nx*mx
      if score < _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE:
         _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE=score
         _LBFGS_BEST_SEQ=a.copy()
    # gradient
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fw), L)[:n]
    ga=2.0*corr
    alpha=float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gf=a*(ga - alpha)
    grad=gf[:-1].copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): grad=np.zeros(n-1)
    if not np.isfinite(val): val=1e300
    return val, grad
```
Use no lambda. Good.

But there is a global `_LBFGS_BEST_SEQ` initially None. If `_LBFGS_BEST_SCORE` is inf, update. In `_refine_lbfgs`, set to initial finite.

`_softmax_restricted`:
```
def _softmax_restricted(phi,n):
    if n<=1: return np.ones(1)
    x=np.empty(n)
    phi=np.asarray(phi).ravel()
    if phi.size < n-1:
       padded=np.zeros(n-1); m=min(...); padded[:m]=phi; phi=padded
    x[:-1]=phi[:n-1]; x[-1]=0.0
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    mx=np.max(x)
    x=x-mx
    ex=np.exp(x)
    s=ex.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0:
        ex=np.ones(n)
        s=n
    return ex/s
```
Good.

```
def _refine_lbfgs(a, deadline, lam=5000.0, maxiter=120):
    global ...
    a=_normalize_array(a)
    n=a.size
    if n<2 or _scipy_minimize is None:
        return a
    init_sc=_score_fast(a)
    _LBFGS_N=n; _LBFGS_LAM=lam; _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE=init_sc; _LBFGS_BEST_SEQ=a.copy()
    phi=_lbfgs_init_phi(a)
    now=time.monotonic()
    rem=deadline-now
    if rem<5: return a
    iters=int(min(maxiter, max(30, rem*80)))  # assume 12ms/call? maybe
    maxfun=int(min(3*iters, max(60, rem*200)))
    options={'maxiter':iters, 'maxfun':maxfun, 'maxls':20, 'maxcor':20, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'disp':False}
    try:
       _scipy_minimize(_lbfgs_fgv, phi, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options=options)
    except Exception:
       pass
    best=_LBFGS_BEST_SEQ
    if best is None: best=a
    best=_normalize_array(best)
    # If best_score worse than init? already init.
    return best
```

```
def _coord_eval_samples(xs, ck, coeff, const, mid, max_unaff, D, n):
    xs=np.asarray(xs,float)
    valid finite etc
    if none: return inf,nan
    aff = ck[None,:] + coeff[None,:]*xs[:,None] + const[None,:]
    aff[:, mid] += xs*xs
    max_aff=np.max(aff, axis=1)
    maxc=np.maximum(max_unaff, max_aff)
    S=D+xs
    vals=np.full(xs.size, inf)
    valid=S>1e-15
    vals[valid]=2.0*n*maxc[valid]/(S[valid]*S[valid])
    idx=int(np.argmin(vals))
    return float(xs[idx]), float(vals[idx])
```
No closures. Good.

In `_coord_update`, after best from grid, refine local and call same.

```
def _add_candidate(cand_list, seq, score, limit=50):
    if seq is None: return
    a=_normalize_array(seq)
    if a.size==0: return
    if score is None or not np.isfinite(score): score=_score_fast(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score): return
    cand_list.append([float(score), int(a.size), a])
    cand_list.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    while len(cand_list)>limit: cand_list.pop()
```

```
def _scan_fourier_starts(n, rng, k=10, deadline=None, freqs=(1,2,3,4), amps=(0.3,0.6,0.9,1.0), phase_count=12):
    out=[]
    if n<2: return out
    x0=np.arange(n)/float(n)
    x1=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    phases=np.linspace(0.0, 2.0*math.pi, int(phase_count), endpoint=False)
    for x in (x0,x1):
      for freq in freqs:
        theta=2*math.pi*freq*x
        for amp in amps:
          for ph in phases:
            if deadline is not None and time.monotonic()>deadline: return [x[0] for x?]
            y=1.0 + amp*np.sin(theta+ph)
            y=np.clip(y,0,None)
            s=y.sum()
            if s>0:
               p=y/s
               sc=_score_fast(p)
               if np.isfinite(sc): out.append([sc,p])
    out.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    return [p[1]?]
```
Return sequences. If no out, return [].
Memory: storing p arrays for up to 512*3809 ~1.9M floats? 15MB, okay. Could be larger with offsets 2*4*4*12=384, 14.6MB. okay.

Let's draft `_block_random`:
```
def _block_random(n, rng, k=3, alpha=1.0):
    n=int(n)
    if n<=1: return np.ones(max(1,n))
    k= int(min(max(1,k), n))
    w = rng.dirichlet(np.full(k, float(alpha)))
    out = np.zeros(n)
    for b in range(k):
       start=(b*n)//k; end=((b+1)*n)//k
       L=end-start
       if L>0 and w[b]>0:
          out[start:end] = w[b]/float(L)
    return _normalize_array(out)
```
This produces mass w[b] per block. Sum 1 already, but normalize.

`_two_level`:
```
def _two_level(n, frac=0.5):
   n=int(n)
   if n<=1: return np.ones(n)
   m=int(round(n*frac))
   m=max(1,min(n-1,m))
   p=np.zeros(n)
   p[:m]=frac/float(m)
   p[m:]=(1-frac)/float(n-m)
   return _normalize_array(p)
```
If frac near1, okay.

`_poly_start`:
```
def _poly_start(n, rng, deg=4):
    if n<=1: return ones
    x=np.linspace(-1,1,n)
    p=np.ones(n)
    for d in range(1,deg+1):
        mon=x**d
        p = p + float(rng.normal(0,0.5))*(mon - np.mean(mon))
    p=np.clip(p,0,None)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: p=np.ones(n)
    return p/s
```

`_anti_mirror_start`: Need if n=2, npairs=1.
```
def _anti_mirror_start(n, rng, alpha=1.0, mode=0):
    n=int(n); p=np.zeros(n)
    if n<=1: return np.ones(n)
    npairs=n//2
    has_mid=n%2==1
    N=1 if npairs==0 and has_mid else npairs + 1
    if N<=0: return np.ones(n)
    W=rng.dirichlet(np.full(N, float(alpha)))
    for r in range(npairs):
       w=W[r]
       if w<=0: continue
       left=r; right=n-1-r
       side=0
       if mode==0: side=1 if rng.random()<0.5 else 0
       elif mode==1: side=r%2
       elif mode==2: side=0
       elif mode==3: side=1 if (r*2654435761) % max(1,(npairs+1)) < (npairs+1)//2 else 0
       else:
          if npairs>1: side=0 if np.cos(2*math.pi*r/float(npairs))>0 else 1
          else: side=0
       if side==0: p[left]=w
       else: p[right]=w
    if has_mid:
       p[npairs] = W[npairs]
    return _normalize_array(p)
```
If W length npairs+1, middle index npairs. Good.

`_make_initial_pool`: For full, random arrays of length n allocate; okay. If `deadline` passed and too late, stop. Use `rng` may be expensive. We'll generate maybe 20. Need include `uniform` and some deterministic.
```
def _make_initial_pool(n, rng, limit=15, deadline=None):
    out=[]
    if n<=1: return [np.ones(n)]
    _append_pool(out, np.ones(n), deadline)
    for frac in (0.25,0.4,0.5,0.6,0.75): _append_pool(out,_two_level(n,frac),deadline)
    for k in (2,3,4,5,8): _append_pool(out,_block_random(n,rng,k,1.0),deadline)
    for mode in range(5): _append_pool(out,_anti_mirror_start(n,rng,1.0,mode),deadline)
    for alpha in (0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0): _append_pool(out, rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, alpha)), deadline)
    _append_pool(out, np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)), deadline)
    _append_pool(out, np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.3,n)), deadline)
    for dens in (0.3,0.5,0.7): _append_pool(out, (rng.random(n)<dens).astype(float), deadline)
    for _ in range(4): _append_pool(out,_poly_start(n,rng,4),deadline)
    out.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    res=[a for sc,a in out[:max(1,limit)]]
    if not res: res=[np.ones(n)/n]
    return res
```
For n=3809, generating Dirichlet 4 times okay. Binary 3.

`_scan_fourier_starts`: use `_append_pool`? It returns scored list. Could reuse out and sort.
```
def _scan_fourier_starts(n, rng, k=10, deadline=None, freqs=(1,2,3,4), amps=(0.3,0.55,0.8,1.0), phase_count=12):
   out=[]
   if n<2: return []
   x0=np.arange(n)/float(n)
   x1=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
   phase=np.linspace(0,2π,phase_count,endpoint=False)
   for x in (x0,x1):
     for f in freqs:
       theta=2π f x
       for amp in amps:
         for ph in phase:
            _append_pool(out, 1+amp*np.sin(theta+ph), deadline)
   out.sort(key=_candidate_key)
   return [a for sc,a in out[:max(1,k)]]
```
This will score uniform for amp=0? amps start0.3. Good. If y negative small due roundoff, clip. Good.

def _coordinate_polish(p, deadline, rng, grid_count=24, refine_count=10, max_perturb=4):
    p=_normalize_array(p)
    n=p.size
    if n<2 or deadline <= time.monotonic(): return p
    c=np.convolve(p,p); c=np.clip(c,0,None); score=2*n*max(c)
    best=p.copy(); bestscore=score
    stagnant=0; cycles=0; perts=0
    scales=[0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05]
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.5:
       order=rng.permutation(n)
       improved=False
       for i in order:
          if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.2: break
          p,c,score,imp=_coord_update(p,c,score,int(i),grid_count,refine_count)
          if imp: improved=True
       # recompute direct
       p=_normalize_array(p)
       if p.size==0: break
       c=np.convolve(p,p); c=np.clip(c,0,None); score=2*n*float(max(c)) if c.size else inf
       if not finite: break
       err=1e-12*max(1,bestscore)
       if score < bestscore - err:
          best=p.copy(); bestscore=score; cycles=0; stagnant=0
       else:
          cycles += 1
          if not improved:
             stagnant += 1
          else:
             stagnant=0
       if cycles > 8:
          break
       if not improved:
          if perts < max_perturb and stagnant >=2:
             scale=scales[min(perts, len(scales)-1)]
             pert = best * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, scale, n))
             p=_normalize_array(np.clip(pert,0,None))
             if p.size>0:
                c=np.convolve(p,p); sc=...
                if sc < bestscore: update; stagnant=0
             perts +=1
          elif stagnant >=6:
             break
       # If p differs from best? continue with current p if improved else maybe set to best.
       if score >= bestscore * 1.05:
           p=best.copy()
           c=np.convolve(p,p); score=bestscore
    return best
```

def _smooth_fourier_start(n, rng, modes=3):
   x=np.arange(n)/n
   y=np.ones(n)

```python
"""
Heuristic minimizer for 2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a))**2 with a >= 0.

This version emphasizes:
  * structured analytic starts (Fourier sweeps, anti-mirror, blocks, random
    smooth densities),
  * fast FFT-based log-sum-exp gradient polishing via scipy L-BFGS-B when
    available,
  * exact coordinate descent on the normalized sequence,
  * multi-resolution prototype generation and smooth expansion to length 3809.
"""

import math
import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _SCIPY_MINIMIZE
except Exception:
    _SCIPY_MINIMIZE = None

# Globals used by the L-BFGS callback so that all helpers remain top-level.
_LBFGS_N = 0
_LBFGS_LAM = 1000.0
_LBFGS_BEST_SCORE = float("inf")
_LBFGS_BEST_SEQ = None


def _clean_array(arr):
    if arr is None:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a


def _normalize_array(arr):
    a = _clean_array(arr)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(a.size if a.size > 0 else 1, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = max(1, int(m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _convolve_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(a[0] * a[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 64:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
        c = np.clip(c, 0.0, None)
        return c
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.clip(c, 0.0, None)
    return c


def _score_fast(seq):
    a = _normalize_array(seq)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    if n == 1:
        return 2.0
    c = _convolve_fft(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, mx))


def _score_direct(seq):
    a = _normalize_array(seq)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    if n == 1:
        return 2.0
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, mx))


def _try_external_score(seq):
    a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if a.size > 12000:
        return _score_fast(a)
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a])
        if np.isfinite(val):
            return float(val)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return _score_fast(a)


def _candidate_key(cand):
    return cand[0]


def _add_candidate(cand_list, seq, score, limit=60):
    if seq is None:
        return
    a = _normalize_array(seq)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    if score is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        score = _score_fast(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    cand_list.append([float(score), int(a.size), a])
    cand_list.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    while len(cand_list) > limit:
        cand_list.pop()


def _get_best_candidate(cand_list):
    if not cand_list:
        return None
    cand = min(cand_list, key=_candidate_key)
    return _normalize_array(cand[2])


def _append_pool(pool, seq, deadline=None):
    if seq is None:
        return
    if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
        return
    a = _normalize_array(seq)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    sc = _score_fast(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        pool.append([float(sc), a])


def _two_level(n, frac=0.5):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    m = int(round(n * float(frac)))
    m = max(1, min(n - 1, m))
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[:m] = float(frac) / float(m)
    p[m:] = max(0.0, 1.0 - float(frac)) / float(n - m)
    return _normalize_array(p)


def _block_random(n, rng, k=3, alpha=1.0):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(min(max(1, k), n))
    try:
        w = rng.dirichlet(np.full(k, float(alpha), dtype=np.float64))
    except Exception:
        w = np.full(k, 1.0 / k, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for b in range(k):
        s = (b * n) // k
        e = ((b + 1) * n) // k
        L = e - s
        if L > 0 and w[b] > 0.0:
            out[s:e] = float(w[b]) / float(L)
    return _normalize_array(out)


def _anti_mirror_start(n, rng, alpha=1.0, mode=0):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    npairs = n // 2
    has_mid = (n % 2 == 1)
    count = npairs + (1 if has_mid else 0)
    if count <= 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        W = rng.dirichlet(np.full(count, float(alpha), dtype=np.float64))
    except Exception:
        W = np.full(count, 1.0 / count, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for r in range(npairs):
        w = float(W[r])
        if w <= 0.0:
            continue
        left = r
        right = n - 1 - r
        side = 0
        if mode == 0:
            side = 1 if rng.random() < 0.5 else 0
        elif mode == 1:
            side = r % 2
        elif mode == 2:
            side = 0
        elif mode == 3:
            side = 1 if (r * 2654435761) % max(1, npairs + 1) < max(1, (npairs + 1) // 2) else 0
        else:
            if npairs > 1:
                side = 0 if np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * r / float(npairs)) > 0.0 else 1
            else:
                side = 0
        if side == 0:
            p[left] = w
        else:
            p[right] = w
    if has_mid:
        p[npairs] = float(W[npairs])
    return _normalize_array(p)


def _poly_start(n, rng, deg=4):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for d in range(1, int(deg) + 1):
        mono = x ** d
        p = p + float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5)) * (mono - np.mean(mono))
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return p / s


def _smooth_fourier_start(n, rng, modes=3, max_amp=1.0):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, int(modes) + 1):
        amp = float(max_amp) * (1.0 / float(m + 0.5)) * rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
        th = 2.0 * math.pi * float(m) * x + ph
        p = p + amp * np.sin(th)
        if rng.random() < 0.7:
            p = p + 0.5 * amp * np.cos(th)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return p / s


def _make_initial_pool(n, rng, limit=18, deadline=None):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return [np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)]
    pool = []
    _append_pool(pool, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), deadline)
    for frac in (0.25, 0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.75):
        _append_pool(pool, _two_level(n, frac), deadline)
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
    for k in (2, 3, 4, 5, 8):
        _append_pool(pool, _block_random(n, rng, k, 1.0), deadline)
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
    for mode in range(5):
        _append_pool(pool, _anti_mirror_start(n, rng, 1.0, mode), deadline)
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
    for alpha in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        _append_pool(pool, rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, float(alpha), dtype=np.float64)), deadline)
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
    _append_pool(pool, np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)), deadline)
    _append_pool(pool, np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.3, n)), deadline)
    for dens in (0.30, 0.50, 0.70):
        _append_pool(pool, (rng.random(n) < dens).astype(np.float64), deadline)
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
    for _ in range(4):
        _append_pool(pool, _poly_start(n, rng, 4), deadline)
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
    for _ in range(5):
        _append_pool(pool, _smooth_fourier_start(n, rng, 3, 1.0), deadline)
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
    pool.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    out = [a for sc, a in pool[:max(1, int(limit))]]
    if not out:
        out = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]
    return out


def _scan_fourier_starts(n, rng, k=14, deadline=None,
                         freqs=(1, 2, 3, 4),
                         amps=(0.30, 0.55, 0.80, 1.00),
                         phase_count=12):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return []
    pool = []
    x0 = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    x1 = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    phases = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi, int(phase_count), endpoint=False, dtype=np.float64)
    for x in (x0, x1):
        for f in freqs:
            theta = 2.0 * math.pi * float(f) * x
            for amp in amps:
                for ph in phases:
                    if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
                        break
                    y = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.sin(theta + float(ph))
                    _append_pool(pool, y, deadline)
                    if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
                        break
                if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    break
            if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

    # A small two-harmonic sweep, which often helps escape one-signal basins.
    if deadline is None or time.monotonic() <= deadline:
        th1 = 2.0 * math.pi * x0
        th2 = 4.0 * math.pi * x0
        ph1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi, 6, endpoint=False, dtype=np.float64)
        ph2 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi, 6, endpoint=False, dtype=np.float64)
        for a1 in (0.60, 0.85, 1.00):
            for p1 in ph1:
                base = 1.0 + float(a1) * np.sin(th1 + float(p1))
                for a2 in (0.20, 0.35, 0.55):
                    for p2 in ph2:
                        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
                            break
                        y = base + float(a2) * np.sin(th2 + float(p2))
                        _append_pool(pool, y, deadline)
                        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
                            break
                    if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
                        break
                if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    break
            if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            return []

    pool.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    out = [a for sc, a in pool[:max(1, int(k))]]
    if not out:
        out = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]
    return out


def _expand_to_length_smooth(proto, target_len):
    proto = _normalize_array(proto)
    m = int(proto.size)
    target_len = int(target_len)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, target_len), dtype=np.float64)
    if target_len <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == target_len:
        return proto.copy()
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(target_len, float(proto[0] / target_len), dtype=np.float64)

    # Treat prototype as probability mass on m cells and interpolate density.
    density_old = proto * float(m)
    x_old = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
    x_new = (np.arange(target_len, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(target_len)
    density_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, density_old)
    a = np.clip(density_new, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        # Fallback: block expansion.
        out = np.zeros(target_len, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = 0
        for i in range(m):
            L0 = idx
            idx += int(round(target_len * float(i + 1) / float(m))) - L0
            if L0 < idx:
                out[L0:idx] = float(proto[i]) / float(idx - L0)
        a = out
        s = float(np.sum(a))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            return np.ones(target_len, dtype=np.float64) / float(target_len)
    return a / s


def _softmax_restricted(phi, n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    phi = np.asarray(phi, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if phi.size != n - 1:
        tmp = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(phi.size, n - 1)
        if m > 0:
            tmp[:m] = phi[:m]
        phi = tmp
    x = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x[:-1] = phi
    x[-1] = 0.0
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(x))
    x = x - mx
    ex = np.exp(x)
    s = float(np.sum(ex))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        ex = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    return ex / s


def _lbfgs_init_phi(a):
    a = _normalize_array(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    eps = 1e-300
    a = np.clip(a, eps, None)
    la = np.log(a)
    return np.asarray(la[:-1] - la[-1], dtype=np.float64)


def _lbfgs_fgv(phi):
    global _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE, _LBFGS_BEST_SEQ

    n = int(_LBFGS_N)
    lam = float(_LBFGS_LAM)
    if n <= 1:
        return 2.0, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if lam <= 0.0:
        lam = 1.0

    phi = np.asarray(phi, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = _softmax_restricted(phi, n)

    required = max(2 * n - 1, 3 * n - 2)
    L = _next_pow2(required)

    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.clip(c, 0.0, None)

    if c.size == 0:
        return 1e300, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if np.isfinite(mx):
        exscore = float(2.0 * n * mx)
        if exscore < _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE:
            _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE = exscore
            _LBFGS_BEST_SEQ = a.copy()

    x = lam * c
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e300, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    m = float(np.max(x))
    ex = np.exp(x - m)
    s = float(np.sum(ex))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        ex = np.ones_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(c.size)
    w = ex / s

    # Objective scaled to the same order as the final score.
    val = float((2.0 * n) * (m + math.log(max(s, 1e-300))) / lam)

    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conjugate(fw), L)[:n]
    grad_a = 2.0 * corr
    alpha = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_full = a * (grad_a - alpha)
    grad = grad_full[:-1].copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e300
    return val, grad


def _refine_lbfgs(seq, deadline, lam=5000.0, maxiter=120):
    global _LBFGS_N, _LBFGS_LAM, _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE, _LBFGS_BEST_SEQ

    a = _normalize_array(seq)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 2 or _SCIPY_MINIMIZE is None:
        return a
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = float(deadline) - now
    if rem < 5.0:
        return a

    init_score = _score_fast(a)
    _LBFGS_N = n
    _LBFGS_LAM = float(lam)
    _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE = float(init_score)
    _LBFGS_BEST_SEQ = a.copy()

    phi0 = _lbfgs_init_phi(a)
    iters = int(min(max(30, rem * 80), max(30, int(maxiter))))
    maxfun = int(min(3 * iters, max(60, rem * 200)))

    options = {
        "maxiter": iters,
        "maxfun": maxfun,
        "maxls": 20,
        "maxcor": 20,
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "gtol": 1e-8,
        "disp": False,
    }

    try:
        _SCIPY_MINIMIZE(_lbfgs_fgv, phi0, jac=True, method="L-BFGS-B", options=options)
    except Exception:
        pass

    best = _LBFGS_BEST_SEQ
    if best is None:
        best = a
    best = _normalize_array(best)

    # Keep the entry around only if it is still finite.
    if _score_fast(best) >= float("inf"):
        best = a
    return best


def _coord_eval_samples(xs, ck, coeff, const, mid, max_unaff, D, n):
    xs = np.asarray(xs, dtype=np.float64)
    xs = xs[np.isfinite(xs) & (xs >= 0.0) & (xs <= 1.0e6)]
    if xs.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), float("nan")

    aff = ck[None, :] + coeff[None, :] * xs[:, None] + const[None, :]
    aff[:, mid] += xs * xs
    max_aff = np.max(aff, axis=1)
    maxc = np.maximum(float(max_unaff), max_aff)
    maxc = np.maximum(0.0, maxc)
    S = D + xs
    vals = np.full(xs.size, float("inf"), dtype=np.float64)
    valid = S > 1e-15
    vals[valid] = (2.0 * int(n)) * maxc[valid] / (S[valid] * S[valid])

    idx = int(np.argmin(vals))
    return float(xs[idx]), float(vals[idx])


def _coordinate_update(p, c, score, i, grid_count, refine_count):
    n = int(p.size)
    old = float(p[i])
    if not np.isfinite(old) or old < 0.0:
        old = 0.0
    D = 1.0 - old
    if D < 0.0:
        D = 0.0

    k0 = int(i)
    k1 = k0 + n
    ck = c[k0:k1]

    coeff = 2.0 * p.copy()
    const = -2.0 * old * p.copy()
    mid = k0
    coeff[mid] = 0.0
    const[mid] = -old * old

    max_unaff = 0.0
    if k0 > 0:
        m0 = float(np.max(c[:k0]))
        if np.isfinite(m0) and m0 > max_unaff:
            max_unaff = m0
    if k1 < c.size:
        m1 = float(np.max(c[k1:]))
        if np.isfinite(m1) and m1 > max_unaff:
            max_unaff = m1

    gc = max(8, int(grid_count))
    if old > 0.0:
        ratios = np.logspace(-10.0, 10.0, gc, dtype=np.float64)
        xs = old * ratios
    else:
        xs = np.logspace(-10.0, 3.0, gc, dtype=np.float64)
    xs = np.concatenate((xs, np.array([0.0, old], dtype=np.float64)))

    best_x, best_val = _coord_eval_samples(xs, ck, coeff, const, mid, max_unaff, D, n)
    tol = 1e-12 * max(1.0, float(score))
    if not np.isfinite(best_val) or best_val >= float(score) - tol:
        return p, c, float(score), False

    rn = max(0, int(refine_count))
    if rn > 0:
        if int(np.searchsorted(xs, best_x)) > 0:
            left = float(xs[max(0, int(np.searchsorted(xs, best_x)) - 1)])
        else:
            left = 0.0
        if int(np.searchsorted(xs, best_x)) < xs.size - 1:
            right = float(xs[min(int(np.searchsorted(xs, best_x)) + 1, xs.size - 1)])
        else:
            right = best_x * 10.0 if best_x > 0.0 else float(max(xs[-1], 1.0e-12))

        if best_x > 0.0:
            local = best_x * np.logspace(-1.0, 1.0, rn, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            hi = right if right > 0.0 else 1e-12
            local = np.linspace(0.0, hi, rn, dtype=np.float64)
        local = np.concatenate((local, np.array([left, best_x, right], dtype=np.float64)))
        local = np.unique(local)

        rx, rv = _coord_eval_samples(local, ck, coeff, const, mid, max_unaff, D, n)
        if np.isfinite(rv) and rv < best_val:
            best_x, best_val = rx, rv

    if not np.isfinite(best_val) or best_val >= float(score) - tol:
        return p, c, float(score), False

    S = D + best_x
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return p, c, float(score), False
    S2 = S * S

    c_new = c / S2
    aff_best = ck + coeff * best_x + const
    aff_best[mid] += best_x * best_x
    c_new[k0:k1] = aff_best / S2

    p_new = p / S
    p_new[i] = best_x / S

    p_new = np.clip(p_new, 0.0, None)
    c_new = np.clip(c_new, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(p_new))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return p, c, float(score), False
    p_new = p_new / s
    c_new = c_new / (s * s)
    p_new = np.clip(p_new, 0.0, None)
    c_new = np.clip(c_new, 0.0, None)

    s2 = float(np.sum(p_new))
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 0.0:
        return p, c, float(score), False
    p_new = p_new / s2
    c_new = c_new / (s2 * s2)

    if c_new.size == 0:
        return p, c, float(score), False
    score_new = float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(np.max(c_new))))
    if not np.isfinite(score_new) or score_new >= float(score) - tol:
        return p, c, float(score), False
    return p_new, c_new, score_new, True


def _coordinate_polish(seq, deadline, rng, grid_count=24, refine_count=10, max_perturb=4):
    p = _normalize_array(seq)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 2:
        return p
    deadline = float(deadline)
    if deadline <= time.monotonic():
        return p

    c = np.convolve(p, p)
    c = np.clip(c, 0.0, None)
    score = float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(np.max(c)))) if c.size else float("inf")

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_score = score

    stagnant = 0
    cycles_since_best = 0
    perts = 0
    scales = (0.55, 0.28, 0.12, 0.05)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.5:
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        improved = False
        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.2:
                break
            p, c, score, imp = _coordinate_update(p, c, score, int(i), grid_count, refine_count)
            if imp:
                improved = True

        p = _normalize_array(p)
        if p.size != n:
            break
        c = np.convolve(p, p)
        c = np.clip(c, 0.0, None)
        score = float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(np.max(c)))) if c.size else float("inf")
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            break

        tol = 1e-12 * max(1.0, float(best_score))
        if score < best_score - tol:
            best_score = score
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_c = c.copy()
            cycles_since_best = 0
        else:
            cycles_since_best += 1

        if improved:
            stagnant = 0
        else:
            stagnant += 1

        if cycles_since_best >= 6 and score > best_score * 1.02:
            p = best_p.copy()
            c = best_c.copy()
            score = best_score
            cycles_since_best = 0

        if not improved:
            if stagnant >= 2 and perts < max_perturb:
                scale = scales[min(perts, len(scales) - 1)]
                pert = best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(scale), n))
                pert = np.clip(pert, 0.0, None)
                sp = float(np.sum(pert))
                if np.isfinite(sp) and sp > 0.0:
                    p = pert / sp
                    c = np.convolve(p, p)
                    c = np.clip(c, 0.0, None)
                    score = float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, float(np.max(c)))) if c.size else float("inf")
                    if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_score = score
                        best_p = p.copy()
                        best_c = c.copy()
                        cycles_since_best = 0
                perts += 1
                stagnant = 0
            elif stagnant >= 7:
                break

        if score > best_score * 1.25 + 1e-8:
            p = best_p.copy()
            c = best_c.copy()
            score = best_score

    return best_p


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    global _LBFGS_BEST_SCORE, _LBFGS_BEST_SEQ

    seed = 42 if seed is None else int(seed)
    budget = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(30.0, budget - 60.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    final_len = 3809
    candidates = []

    print(f"[init] seed={seed}, budget={budget:.1f}s, target final length={final_len}")

    # Seed with any previously found sequence if available.
    try:
        hs = _clean_array(height_sequence_1)
        if hs.size > 0:
            sc = _score_fast(hs)
            _add_candidate(candidates, hs, sc, limit=80)
            print(f"[history] existing height_sequence score={sc:.12f}, len={hs.size}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Guaranteed fallback of the right length.
    fallback = _two_level(final_len, 0.5)
    fb = _score_fast(fallback)
    _add_candidate(candidates, fallback, fb, limit=80)
    print(f"[fallback] two-level half-block score={fb:.12f}")

    # If time is extremely small, return current best.
    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 25.0:
        best = _get_best_candidate(candidates)
        if best is None:
            best = fallback
        return [float(x) for x in _normalize_array(best)]

    # Full-length structured starts.
    fourier_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + 150.0)
    for a in _scan_fourier_starts(final_len, rng, k=18, deadline=fourier_deadline):
        _add_candidate(candidates, a, None, limit=80)
    for a in _make_initial_pool(final_len, rng, limit=22, deadline=min(deadline, t0 + 220.0)):
        _add_candidate(candidates, a, None, limit=80)

    # Prototype pool: Fourier + random, expanded to final length and also kept short.
    proto_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + 700.0)
    proto_lengths = (16, 32, 64, 128, 256)
    for m in proto_lengths:
        if time.monotonic() >= proto_deadline:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
            break
        kd = min(proto_deadline, time.monotonic() + 45.0)
        for a in _scan_fourier_starts(m, rng, k=8, deadline=kd):
            _add_candidate(candidates, a, None, limit=80)
            _add_candidate(candidates, _expand_to_length_smooth(a, final_len), None, limit=80)
        pd = min(proto_deadline, time.monotonic() + 45.0)
        for a in _make_initial_pool(m, rng, limit=10, deadline=pd):
            _add_candidate(candidates, a, None, limit=80)
            _add_candidate(candidates, _expand_to_length_smooth(a, final_len), None, limit=80)

    # Sort and refine top candidates.
    candidates.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    top = [c for c in candidates[:12]]

    refine_deadline = max(time.monotonic(), deadline - 120.0)
    print(f"[refine] starting full polishing, top fast scores: "
          + ", ".join(f"{c[0]:.10f}" for c in top[:6]))

    for idx, cand in enumerate(top):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= refine_deadline:
            break
        sc, nlen, a = cand
        nlen = int(nlen)
        a = _normalize_array(a)

        # Allocate a modest slice per candidate.
        remaining = max(10.0, refine_deadline - now)
        alloc = max(25.0, min(320.0, remaining / max(1, len(top) - idx)))
        slice_end = min(refine_deadline, now + alloc)

        p = a.copy()

        # Two-pass log-sum-exp polishing if SciPy is available.
        if nlen > 1 and slice_end - now > 25.0:
            lam_end = min(slice_end, now + min(80.0, alloc * 0.45))
            p = _refine_lbfgs(p, lam_end, lam=2000.0, maxiter=120)
        if nlen > 1 and slice_end - time.monotonic() > 25.0:
            lam_end2 = min(slice_end, time.monotonic() + min(90.0, alloc * 0.45))
            p = _refine_lbfgs(p, lam_end2, lam=8000.0, maxiter=100)

        # Exact coordinate descent to reduce the true max coefficient.
        if slice_end - time.monotonic() > 8.0:
            if nlen <= 64:
                gc, rc = 44, 16
            elif nlen <= 128:
                gc, rc = 36, 12
            elif nlen <= 512:
                gc, rc = 30, 12
            elif nlen <= 2048:
                gc, rc = 25, 10
            else:
                gc, rc = 22, 9
            p = _coordinate_polish(p, slice_end - 2.0, rng, grid_count=gc, refine_count=rc, max_perturb=4)

        _add_candidate(candidates, p, None, limit=80)
        if idx < 6:
            print(f"[polish] candidate {idx} len={nlen} fast={_score_fast(p):.12f}")

    # One final long polish on the current best.
    best = _get_best_candidate(candidates)
    if best is None:
        best = fallback
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 40.0:
        nbest = int(best.size)
        if nbest <= 64:
            gc, rc = 50, 18
        elif nbest <= 128:
            gc, rc = 40, 14
        elif nbest <= 512:
            gc, rc = 32, 12
        elif nbest <= 2048:
            gc, rc = 26, 10
        else:
            gc, rc = 22, 9
        best = _coordinate_polish(best, deadline - 15.0, rng,
                                  grid_count=gc, refine_count=rc, max_perturb=3)
        _add_candidate(candidates, best, None, limit=80)

    # Final careful selection using the authoritative evaluator when reasonable.
    candidates.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    top = [c for c in candidates[:20] if c[1] <= 12000]
    if not top:
        top = [c for c in candidates[:5]]
    if not top:
        chosen = fallback
    else:
        chosen = None
        best_ext = float("inf")
        for c in top:
            seq = _normalize_array(c[2])
            ext = _try_external_score(seq)
            if np.isfinite(ext) and ext < best_ext:
                best_ext = ext
                chosen = seq
        if chosen is None:
            chosen = _get_best_candidate(candidates)
            if chosen is None:
                chosen = fallback

    chosen = _normalize_array(chosen)
    # Ensure a safe, finite, nonnegative, sum-normalized output.
    out = np.asarray(chosen, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(out))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-6:
        out = np.ones(max(1, int(out.size)), dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(out.size)
    out = out / s

    final_score = _try_external_score(out)
    print(f"[final] returned length={out.size}, score={final_score:.12f}")
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
